========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. I hope I'm not taking up too much of your time. Um, I'm here because... well, things at the lab have been a little... scattered lately. I'm a physical scientist, and usually I'm very on top of things, I promise. But recently, I just can't seem to keep my mind on the actual experiments I'm supposed to be running. 

Like, I'll be in the middle of a really important grant call or a safety meeting, and I'll just... notice things. A slight vibration in one of the vacuum pumps, or a student whispering something in the hallway, or I'll remember some odd peak in a spectrum from three years ago. And suddenly, I'm just off chasing that down for hours. It's not that I don't care about the main project, it's just my brain gets so pulled away. And then the things everyone actually needs from me kind of... fall apart. Is that something you can help with? I really want to get back to being a good team member.

It's just that I get so interested in the new detail that I don't even think to stop myself and try to ignore it. I really hope it doesn't seem like I'm trying to run away from the hard parts of my job, or my life. I really do try my best to stay on top of everything and be reliable... is that the kind of approach you'd expect someone to have? I just want to make sure I'm handling things the right way.

Oh, um... no, I wouldn't say it's about control. I really don't feel like I have any control issues or anything like that. 

I mean, I'm very comfortable working in a team and letting other people take the lead. I don't need to micromanage my environment to feel secure, you know? I'm actually pretty adaptable. When a detail catches my attention, it's just... well, just me being thorough, I think. Not me trying to dominate the project or clinging to control.

I hope it doesn't come across like I'm trying to control everything around me? I just really value being cooperative and easy to work with.

I can usually just put things out of my mind if I need to focus on my work.\n\nI hope that's the right answer? I just try to stay positive and focused on the present. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, goodness, no. Um... I really don't think so. I mean, I don't feel like I'm overly anxious or stressed about my work, honestly. 

I know my job can be demanding sometimes, but I actually feel like I handle stress pretty well. I don't feel the need to escape or hide from it.
